- **find-image**: Given a plain-language description, find the best-matching open-source illustration on undraw.co, recolour it to the book's Rosely palette, save it, and (if a section is named) place it in the book. Use whenever the user wants to "find an image/illustration/graphic for" a spot, "add a picture to §X", "get an undraw image of …", or otherwise illustrate a passage. Because an exact match is unlikely, the skill is deliberately resilient: it tries several search terms, downloads a shortlist of ~3 candidates, renders a preview of each, looks at them, and picks the one that actually matches the desc… ([ChristineTham/aidou](https://github.com/ChristineTham/aidou/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/find-image) · 2 copies)
- **find-images**: Find images for the user's content (Pinterest for ideas and mood boards, Openverse and Pexels for images cleared to use), download them with a record of where each came from, and check whether each one is usable — rights, size, crop and a visual check. Use when the user wants images, a mood board, Pinterest research, or asks whether an image can be used. ([iammiracle/mskills](https://github.com/iammiracle/mskills/tree/HEAD/content/find-images))
- **find-images**: Find and use existing images with Lightdrift when building websites, presentations, or educational content, or when asked to source photographs or visual references. Search by meaning and retrieve files with source and license details. Use for image retrieval, not image generation. ([JacksonHolland/lightdrift-claude-plugin](https://github.com/JacksonHolland/lightdrift-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/find-images))
- **find-images**: Find images for slides, reports, documents and web pages - real photos (products, places, events, people), free-to-use stock photos, official product images and icons. Compares many candidates on one numbered contact sheet, downloads the chosen originals, records source and licence, and builds a credits page. Use when the user asks for pictures, images or icons for a deck or document, or when a deck being built is text-heavy with few images. 用于配图、找图、搜图、下载图片或图标，或者 PPT 文字多、图片少时。 ([Kishibe999/find-images](https://github.com/Kishibe999/find-images/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
